# Run and deploy your AI Studio app This contains everything you need to run your app locally. View your app in AI Studio: https://ai.studio/apps/8f005d3c-5a01-4ad6-adeb-80419d5a15b5 ## Run Locally **Prerequisites:** Node.js 1. Install dependencies: `npm install` 2. Set the `GEMINI_API_KEY` in [.env.local](.env.local) to your Gemini API key 3. Run the app: `npm run dev` # Security Specification - CMI Office Lunch ## Data Invariants 1. **Menu Items**: Can only be created, updated, or deleted by an authorized administrator. 2. **Orders**: Each order must have a valid `userId` matching the authenticated user. 3. **Settings**: Only administrators can modify global application settings. 4. **General**: All document IDs must be valid strings. ## The Dirty Dozen Payloads (Target: Permission Denied) 1. **Malicious Menu Create**: User without admin role tries to add an expensive item. 2. **Order Spoofing**: User A tries to place an order with `userId` of User B. 3. **Settings Hijack**: Regular user tries to change office name or timing. 4. **Order Deletion**: User tries to delete an old order to avoid record (Orders should be immutable by users). 5. **Menu Price Update**: Non-admin tries to change the price of a lunch item. 6. **Shadow Field Injection**: Adding an `isAdmin: true` field to a user profile or order. 7. **Negative Price**: Creating a lunch item with a negative price. 8. **Invalid ID**: Using a 2KB string as a document ID. 9. **Employee ID Masking**: User trying to submit an order with someone else's employee name but their own UID. 10. **State Shortcut**: Attempting to skip total cost calculation (client-side validation only). 11. **PII Leak**: Non-authorized user trying to list all orders (metadata leak). 12. **Future Timestamp**: Setting a `date` in the future for an order. ## Admin Definition - Users with email `sanaullahkhanyousafzai7@gmail.com` (verified). { "compilerOptions": { }, [employeeInfo.id]); // --- Helpers --- const formatCurrency = (amount: number) => { return `${settings.currency} ${amount.toLocaleString()}`; }; // --- Notifications Helper --- const playNotificationSound = () => { try { const AudioContextClass = window.AudioContext || (window as any).webkitAudioContext; osc1.frequency.setValueAtTime(587.33, now); gain1.gain.setValueAtTime(0, now); gain1.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.12, now + 0.04); gain1.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.35); osc1.connect(gain1); gain1.connect(ctx.destination); osc1.start(now); osc1.stop(now + 0.35); // Chime note 2 (A5) const osc2 = ctx.createOscillator(); const gain2 = ctx.createGain(); osc2.type = 'sine'; osc2.frequency.setValueAtTime(880.00, now + 0.1); gain2.gain.setValueAtTime(0, now + 0.1); gain2.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.18, now + 0.14); gain2.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, now + 0.55); osc2.connect(gain2); gain2.connect(ctx.destination); width = MAX_DIM; } } else { if (height > MAX_DIM) { width = Math.round((width * MAX_DIM) / height); height = MAX_DIM; } } canvas.width = width; canvas.height = height; const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d'); }; img.src = reader.result as string; }; reader.readAsDataURL(file); } }; // --- Actions --- const addToCart = (item: LunchItem) => { setCart(prev => { const existing = prev.find(cartItem => cartItem.id === item.id); }); }; const updateCartQuantity = (id: string, delta: number) => { setCart(prev => { return prev.map(item => { if (item.id === id) { const newQty = item.quantity + delta; return newQty > 0 ? { ...item, quantity: newQty } : item; const newOrder: Order = { id: orderId, date: new Date().toISOString(), employeeName: orderName, employeeId: orderEmpId, userId: user ? user.uid : 'anonymous-kiosk', items: [...cart], total: totalOrderCost, status: 'Pending' }; try { if (user || !isKioskMode || orderEmpId !== 'kiosk-anonymous') { // Wallet logic for authenticated users or kiosk guests who provide an Employee ID await runTransaction(db, async (transaction) => { const empRef = doc(db, 'employees', orderEmpId); } newBalance = empData.balance - totalOrderCost; transaction.update(empRef, { balance: newBalance }); } else { transaction.set(empRef, { id: orderEmpId, name: orderName, balance: newBalance }); } const orderRef = doc(db, 'orders', orderId);
